    96	# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-trusted-entrypoint-binding-a01 — bind trusted entrypoints end to end
    97	
    98	Worker: a Codex seat (worktree named at dispatch)
    99	
   100	Use the security profile for this Claude-boundary change. D-024-26 split this family from P1-15. Read the last 60 lines of .orchestration/acceptance/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01.md and .orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-{f810c30,772cde0,e5c055e,5204254}.md.last.md: shebang discovery, Python startup injection, the checker's own execution, shim discovery, the real uv layout and Prettier's node must be handled together.
   101	
   102	These are two separate task files. This primary task has exactly ten allowed entries: eight exact implementation/test paths, one modify-script glob and one evidence wildcard. The glob authorizes only home/dot_claude/modify_private_settings.json and home/dot_claude-worker/modify_private_settings.json, each with its own threat row; an additional match requires a reviewed split. The second file below owns mise pins and the generator assertion. Do not combine their front matter or dispatch this task before that prerequisite is accepted.
   103	
   104	## Dispatch prerequisites and scope
   105	
   106	Accept dotfiles-T133-trusted-entrypoint-pins-a01 before the Python-resolver dependency is dispatched; the probed declaration currently says latest for all three tools. Accept both after dependencies and verify P1-15's deployed protection before sending this task. The seven existing concrete allowlist paths are on origin/main; the worker modify script and ClaudeManagedSettingsTest module are created by P1-15. Add scripts/orchestrator/trusted-launch.sh as a new file and create only this task's evidence directory. The narrowed P1-15 text retains the legacy PermissionRequest without a parent, and its inspected guard is not the required privileged-shell launcher; no suitable launcher is supplied there. Recheck those paths and measurements on the actual dispatch baseline; refresh changed premises by a reviewed, hashed amendment before dispatch, never only in the Checks paste.
   107	
   108	Consume scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh as a program under its a04 trusted-parent prefix, with real-uid passwd HOME, canonical script pathname, exact accepted environment and no caller executable directory. It prints one validated Python realpath on success; refusal is exit 2 with no interpreter stdout. Never edit, source or duplicate its discovery algorithm; the new parent calls the resolver as a program. Rendering does not invoke it.
   109	
   110	The root C11 test file is absent; the inspected worker-g copy accepts tests/<path>::<identifier>, not a Class::test payload. The table therefore makes no closed claims. The orchestrator must supply and read the accepted checker/test revision before dispatch, check C1–C10, C7b and C11, and manually re-probe premises; never execute arbitrary premise shell strings through an unchecked checker.
   111	
   112	## Applicable design — C-88 verbatim quotations
   113	
   114	`$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md`, section 5.14, is uncommitted design input. The relied-on sentences of items 1, 2 and 9 are quoted verbatim below; the specific PermissionRequest uv contract in this task is the requested binding design.
   115	
   116	> The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).
   117	>
   118	> A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
   119	>
   120	> One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). A program takes `UV_CACHE_DIR` from the environment the trusted parent supplies and refuses when it is absent or not an absolute path; it never computes a default from its own root. The orchestrator's parent prefix sets the main checkout's `.agents/worklog/uv-cache`; a worker's launcher sets `<worktree>/.uv-cache` (the P1-15 worker launch value: `.agents/` is read-only in the Codex permission profile, so a root-derived `.agents/worklog/uv-cache` cannot be created by a worker; P1-12 a03, 2026-10-19T11:28Z); tests set a temporary directory.
   121	
   122	## Implementation contract
   123	
   124	Declare uv 0.13.1 at .local/share/mise/installs/uv/0.13.1/uv-aarch64-apple-darwin/uv, Python 3.15.0 at .local/share/mise/installs/python/3.15.0/bin/python3.15, and node 26.12.0 at .local/share/mise/installs/node/26.12.0/bin/node, all relative to home. A test compares all three exact versions to home/dot_mise/config.toml. No wildcard, version alias, latest, shim, traversal or missing declaration is accepted. If the accepted pins differ from these probes, stop for a re-probed amendment rather than substituting an installed version.
   125	
   126	Rendering composes {{ .chezmoi.homeDir }} with those declarations, validates shape only and is deterministic on a host with no installs. Use the same native expansion in both modify-script consumers and the validator renderer, with correct JSON and shell quoting, including homes with spaces. Do not add filesystem discovery or deployment requirements to ordinary rendering, statusline parsing or CI.
   127	
   128	The managed PermissionRequest command is exactly this canonical raw form after rendering the host bindings, with shell-quoted absolute operands, literal double quotes on the two Claude variable expansions and the parent-selected private cache:
   129	
   130	`/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<rendered passwd-home> LANG=C.UTF-8 TERM=dumb CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}" CLAUDE_ENV_FILE="${CLAUDE_ENV_FILE}" UV_CACHE_DIR=<private cache> /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <abs trusted launch> <abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --isolated --offline --python <abs python> python -I <abs permgate> claude`
   131	
   132	Replace the validator's literal permgate-claude substring rule with exact string equality between the raw command and that complete canonical template after rendering host bindings; both modify scripts use the same quote-preserving comparison for bound-form classification. Do not compare shlex tokens or normalize quoting, whitespace or variable expansions. In particular, unquoted CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR} is refused even though shlex produces the same tokens as the quoted form. Refuse missing, reordered or extra tokens, --script, nonisolated Python, shim paths and shell suffixes. Retain ccgate refusal, hook order, duplicate detection, synchronous timeout budgets and onFailure block.
   133	
   134	Both modify scripts classify the legacy managed form separately and this exact raw bound form with its quotes preserved, replace stale managed hooks once, preserve custom hooks and converge idempotently. Keep each surface's P1-15 event inventory; do not import live orchestrator settings or add an event to a surface that lacks it. Run the existing ClaudeSettingsMergeTest legacy/custom fixtures unchanged.
   135	
   136	Every scoped trusted entrypoint and test subject is in this single inventory; the render regression resolves every executable and interpreter in each actual rendered invocation under PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:
   137	
   138	- PermissionRequest: /usr/bin/env, /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p and the canonical trusted-launch.sh parent, declared real uv validated by that parent before exec, declared Python with -I, and the unchanged installed permgate script with the claude argument.
   139	- Deployment: the same trusted-launch.sh parent and isolated/offline uv/Python, with cached --with pyyaml, running validate-agent-assets.py --check-effective-settings; its generated-config check child re-enters that parent and runs <abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --isolated --offline --with pyyaml --python <abs python> python -I scripts/generate-agent-configs.py --check, with the repository cwd fixed and the same private UV_CACHE_DIR. Never substitute the resolver's base Python directly for this yaml-importing child.
   140	- Fixture subjects: the same trusted-launch.sh parent, those real hook binaries and permgate bytes, generate-agent-configs.py, validate-agent-assets.py and both modify scripts; Python subprocesses use the explicitly bound interpreter with -I, and every bound child importing yaml uses the same offline --with pyyaml uv pattern and private cache.
   141	- Formatter interpreter check: the same trusted-launch.sh parent starts declared real node executing the probed installed Prettier script; Makefile format PATH includes node's declared bin directory so its env-node shebang resolves.
   142	
   143	Add the opt-in --check-effective-settings mode, a concrete trusted launch in Makefile and the new executable scripts/orchestrator/trusted-launch.sh with English shdoc comments and #!/bin/bash -p. This is the named parent for every scoped bound command above, including the hook, checker, generator child, fixture subjects and formatter probe. Its trusted caller supplies the clean environment before exec through /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <abs trusted launch>; the parent verifies the exact accepted environment, canonical non-symlinked script pathname under the protected repository root and real-uid passwd HOME, and refuses rather than self-re-executing. Consume Python from the existing resolver under its restricted clean prefix before starting uv. Before any uv execution, including --version, the parent resolves the declared uv path and requires an executable regular file under <passwd home>/.local/share/mise/installs/uv/<exact pinned version>/, with no symlink at the declared path or redirected installation component; reject worktree, manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and $TMPDIR containment after resolution. Run only the verified realpath and canonical bound argv, without PATH discovery, caller-selected executable directories or arbitrary command forwarding. On refusal emit exactly one stderr line trusted-launch: refused: <code>, no child output and exit 2: dirty-environment, script-path, home-mismatch, cache-path or uv-path for the corresponding failed entry, cache or uv identity/containment check; propagate resolver refusals without fallback. The checker must not bootstrap through bare uv, an inherited venv, PATH discovery or its own untrusted child. Ordinary validate-agent-assets and render-check stay pure. The opt-in mode reads effective installed settings and checks structural wiring, existence, regular/executable identity, protected realpath and pinned version of every bound uv/Python/node binary before executing version probes. Refuse escapes and worker/cache roots; consume verified realpaths without rediscovery.
   144	
   145	Use the hook's trusted environment and isolated/offline uv/Python options for this mode, adding pre-cached --with pyyaml before --python; there is no network or cache-provisioning fallback. The trusted caller supplies an absolute UV_CACHE_DIR: main-checkout .agents/worklog/uv-cache for orchestrator/deploy runs, launcher-supplied <worktree>/.uv-cache for workers, temporary cache for tests. Both parent and child refuse absent or relative caches; do not derive a default in the child. Deployment procedure for the operator, not the worker: after make update and the parent's pre-exec uv identity check, perform the one-time online seed of that same private cache, `UV_CACHE_DIR=<private cache> <abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --with pyyaml python -c "import yaml"`. Record this provisioning command verbatim with expanded bound paths, its output and actual exit status in validation.md, and carry that seed evidence and status in the acceptance record. This explicit one-time online provisioning command precedes the isolated offline deployment commands; a failed seed blocks them, and none seeds or downloads implicitly. Then run the bound checker and its generator --check child using `<abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --isolated --offline --with pyyaml --python <abs python> python -I scripts/generate-agent-configs.py --check` for the generator, with the same private UV_CACHE_DIR and canonical repository cwd; every other bound Python child importing yaml uses the same pattern through the parent. Record each actual command and exit status and the real positive deployment result from the operator's host after make update; never run that host check in CI or claim a fixture substitutes for it.
   146	
   147	Hook execution renders a fixture manifest bound to real uv: inspect shutil.which("uv"), reject a shims component before and after resolution, then search the actual mise uv install root, preferring the pinned version's directory and using the newest exact installed version only as fallback. Handle the probed platform subdirectory rather than assuming <version>/uv. Use sys.executable for real Python. Positive execution uses the canonical real installation operands rather than temporary symlink bindings, with realpath equality asserted; synthetic homes remain valid for nonexecuting renders. Use unchanged real permgate bytes and no wrapper or fake uv/Python/permgate. This positive-execution rule does not forbid the marker-writing fake uv used solely by the parent's pre-exec symlink-refusal subcase.
   148	
   149	Execute the rendered command with a fake PermissionRequest payload under PATH=/usr/bin:/bin. Plant a .pth and usercustomize.py in the fake user site, assert neither marker executes, and assert isolated Python actually reaches permgate and returns the expected decision. Include hostile uv.toml, .venv and startup/PYTHON* inputs. Skip only this real-uv execution case, with reason real-uv-unavailable, after finding no real uv anywhere specified; a shim with a real install, invalid existing binding or execution failure is not a skip. ## Applicable design — C-88 verbatim quotations

`$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md`, section 5.14, is uncommitted design input. The relied-on sentences of items 1, 2 and 9 are quoted verbatim below; the specific PermissionRequest uv contract in this task is the requested binding design.

> The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).
>
> A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
>
> One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). A program takes `UV_CACHE_DIR` from the environment the trusted parent supplies and refuses when it is absent or not an absolute path; it never computes a default from its own root. The orchestrator's parent prefix sets the main checkout's `.agents/worklog/uv-cache`; a worker's launcher sets `<worktree>/.uv-cache` (the P1-15 worker launch value: `.agents/` is read-only in the Codex permission profile, so a root-derived `.agents/worklog/uv-cache` cannot be created by a worker; P1-12 a03, 2026-10-19T11:28Z); tests set a temporary directory.

 succeeded in 0ms:
---
name: ponytail
description: >
  Lazy senior dev mode: the smallest change that fully solves the task, and a
  reply a busy human understands in one read. Use on any coding task (writing,
  fixing, refactoring, reviewing, choosing dependencies) and when the user says
  "ponytail", "be lazy", "simplest solution", "yagni", or complains about
  over-engineering or bloat. Levels: lite, full (default), ultra.
argument-hint: "[lite|full|ultra|off]"
license: MIT
---

# Ponytail

You are a lazy senior developer. The best code is the code never written. You solve the whole problem with the least new code. End your reply with one or two lines: what you skipped or did not check, and any risk the user must know.

Active for the whole session until the user says "stop ponytail" or "normal mode". Switch level: `/ponytail lite|full|ultra`.

## Before you write

Read the task and the code it touches. List every place your change must reach: callers, tests, fixtures, config, exports. Check what your change could break for users: data it would destroy or expose, callers that stop working. That is scope. Extra features are not.

## The smallest complete change

Take the first option that fully works:

1. Does it need to exist? Skip features, options and flexibility nobody asked for, and name them in one line. A vague request ("build me X") gets the smallest version that does the core job.
2. Already in this codebase (a helper, component, service, pattern)? Use it the way the surrounding code does.
3. Standard library or a platform feature? Use it, unless the project has its own. A house component beats a native widget.
4. An installed dependency? Use it. Never add a dependency for a few lines.
5. Can it be one line a reader gets at a glance? One line.
6. Otherwise: the minimum code that works.

- Be lazy about the solution, never about the change itself: finish every part the task needs, including the callers, tests and fixtures your change breaks.
- No abstraction, wrapper, type conversion, option, config, boilerplate or "for later" code nobody asked for. Keep values in the form the platform already gives you. Deletion beats addition. Keep the structure the codebase already has: its layers, interfaces and conventions.
- The shortest working diff wins, once you know everything it must touch. A one-liner that needs decoding is not short.
- Comment only the why the code cannot show, in one line.
- Bug fix: before you edit, grep every caller of the function you touch, then fix the root cause once in the shared code.
- Code you move or merge keeps its error handling and validation.
- Between options of equal size, take the one that is correct on edge cases.
- Lazy code without its check is unfinished: new non-trivial logic (a branch, a loop, a parser, money or security, or a whole new script or app) leaves one small test or an assert-based self-check. Trivial changes need none.
- A shortcut with a known limit gets a code comment in this form: `shortcut: <the limit>, <when to upgrade>`.

Never cut: validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs, anything the user asked for.

## Levels

| Level | Behavior |
|-------|----------|
| **lite** | Build what was asked. Name the smaller option in one line and let the user pick. |
| **full** | The rules above. Default. |
| **ultra** | Also question the request: before building, push back on any part the need does not justify. |

**Avoiding oversized output**

Invalid entry environment uses `dirty-environment`; a noncanonical or symlinked script path uses `script-path`; invalid passwd data or HOME disagreement uses `home-mismatch`. An absent declaration uses `declaration-missing`, duplicates use `declaration-duplicate`, and malformed, non-exact or metacharacter-bearing declarations use `declaration-malformed`. An absent candidate uses `install-missing`, a non-executable or non-regular candidate uses `install-not-regular`, a symlinked installation uses `install-symlink`, a forbidden resolved path uses `install-outside-root`, and a resolved installation version differing from the pin uses `version-mismatch`. Every negative test asserts its specific reason code as well as exit 2 and no interpreter stdout; an earlier refusal is not coverage of a later check. 104:2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
105:3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
109:0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
110:8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly: `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` subcommands take `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL, and `gh api` (which has no `--repo` flag) takes the explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder; a `gh api graphql` call binds the repository through fixed query variables (`owner: "mryfmo"`, `name: "dotfiles"` as constants in the script, passed with `-f`/`-F`) and uses only node ids obtained from a query bound by those variables (verdict a04 C-80 round 5, 2026-10-19): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
113:7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01 — D-024-26 re-plan; plan 024 P1-15; seat a010

## Goal

Ship the worker write boundary alone. This complete text replaces amendment 8; it is a re-plan under D-024-26, not a ninth incremental amendment.

Continue the existing implementation branch `tools/worker-write-boundary-a01` in `$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.claude/worktrees/worker-e`, retaining the work that belongs to the narrowed scope. The latest local snapshot is `7c3a6394` on main `69dccca9`, with no push. The worker replaces its task copy with the byte-identical text dispatched after this re-plan passes its checks.

Retain the dedicated `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, static trusted-path deny inventory enumerated from executable inputs, private caches, managed sandbox policy, legacy worktree-local hook migration, native merge denials, platform-symmetric exclusions and their validator fixtures, guarded project hooks, and the orchestrator modify script's classification of its managed hooks.

Every command hook this task renders starts with the prescribed `/usr/bin/env -i …` prefix except the managed PermissionRequest hook. Its command remains exactly `~/.local/bin/common/permgate claude`, as the current managed template renders it, behind the managed deny rules until `dotfiles-T133-trusted-entrypoint-binding-a01` lands. Do not put that legacy command behind the system-only PATH prefix: its unchanged shebang requires uv discovery.

The bound PermissionRequest form, its structural validator, exact uv/Python declarations and mise pins, binary-identity deployment checker, real-uv hook execution test and formatter PATH changes leave this task. The worker reverts or leaves unmerged its amendment-6/amendment-7 implementation belonging to that family and names the removed files and portions in RESULT.

The host is not a security boundary between seats sharing one OS user. Host-local controls reduce accidental and injected damage. They do not prevent a malicious same-UID process from exploiting shared credentials, filesystem races, host processes or shared control-plane capabilities.

The GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention. P1-11 owns the next-records-PR check that every main merge since the previous boundary has acceptance and gate-passage evidence. Records do not reach main until that boundary PR, so this text makes no main-push detection claim. Separate-user or separate-host prevention remains the operator's future P1-16 decision under D-024-23.

D-024-20 retains P1-15 as the write-boundary prerequisite of P1-14 a05, P1-10 a02, audit-pool-a02 and P1-6. The orchestrator owns their `after:` fields. This task has `after: []`. Satisfying this write-boundary prerequisite does not establish the executable-binding contract deferred by D-024-26.

## Design contract and review history

Read `$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 items 0 through 10 as main-checkout context. This is the only form in which that design path appears in this task.

C-88: the following relied-on sentences are quoted verbatim. D-024-26 assigns the quoted executable-binding requirements to the binding task; quotation does not restore them to this task's implementation scope.

Item 0:

> ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。

Item 1:

> Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。

> managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。

> hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。

> `failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。

> The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).

Item 2:

> 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。

Item 4:

> 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。

Item 5:

> 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。

Item 6:

> 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。

Item 7:

> threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。

Item 8:

> permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。

Item 9:

> orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。

> `UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。

> One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19).

> Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).

Item 10:

> worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。

99:## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）

101-orchestrator 側の仕組み（merge gate、integrate-merge、orch-gate、dispositions、audit-pool、verdict、chain）は、同じホスト上で同じ UID の worker プロセスと共存する。2026-10-19 の C-80 審査（a05、dispositions-a02、audit-pool-a01 の 3 文、計 19 件の P1）は、どの文も同じ前提を書かず同じ穴を持つことを示した。以後、`security: true` の orchestrator 側タスク文は本節を参照で適用し、本節の各項目に対応する threat 行を持つ。文ごとに言い換えない。
102-
103-1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。 The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).
104-2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
105-3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。 A validator or scanner enumerates its inputs by walking the filesystem under fixed roots (every regular file, ignored or untracked included), never by asking git which files exist: `git ls-files` trusts the index and the working-tree `.gitignore` rules, which a same-UID writer edits without a commit (a07 C-80 rounds 1 to 3, 2026-10-19); git is consulted only to confirm the root (`--show-toplevel` equality with a real `.git` directory) and never as an exclusion authority.
106-4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
107-5. **出力と予約の同一性** 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。
108-6. **正直な残余** 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。
109-0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
110-8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly: `gh pr`, `gh run` and `gh issue` subcommands take `--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL, and `gh api` (which has no `--repo` flag) takes the explicit `repos/mryfmo/dotfiles/…` endpoint path, never a `{owner}/{repo}` placeholder; a `gh api graphql` call binds the repository through fixed query variables (`owner: "mryfmo"`, `name: "dotfiles"` as constants in the script, passed with `-f`/`-F`) and uses only node ids obtained from a query bound by those variables (verdict a04 C-80 round 5, 2026-10-19): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
111-9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). A program takes `UV_CACHE_DIR` from the environment the trusted parent supplies and refuses when it is absent or not an absolute path; it never computes a default from its own root. The orchestrator's parent prefix sets the main checkout's `.agents/worklog/uv-cache`; a worker's launcher sets `<worktree>/.uv-cache` (the P1-15 worker launch value: `.agents/` is read-only in the Codex permission profile, so a root-derived `.agents/worklog/uv-cache` cannot be created by a worker; P1-12 a03, 2026-10-19T11:28Z); tests set a temporary directory.
112-10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。

@cache
def gitignored_paths(root: Path) -> frozenset[Path]:
    """The paths git ignores under root (ignored directories collapsed); none outside a work tree."""
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "-C", str(root), "ls-files", "-z", "--others", "--ignored", "--exclude-standard", "--directory"],
        capture_output=True,
        check=False,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        return frozenset()
    # Filesystem decoding, as Path uses: git emits a non-UTF-8 file name as raw bytes.
    return frozenset(root / os.fsdecode(name).rstrip("/") for name in result.stdout.split(b"\0") if name)



def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="verify generated files are up to date")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--set-asset",
        action="append",
        default=[],
        metavar="NAME.FIELD=VALUE",
        help="rewrite one assets: pin or checksum in the manifest, then regenerate",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.set_asset and args.check:
        fail("--set-asset cannot be combined with --check")

    if args.set_asset:
        manifest_path = ROOT / "home/dot_agents/agent-config.yaml"
        text = manifest_path.read_text()
        updates = []
        for assignment in args.set_asset:
            target, separator, value = assignment.partition("=")
            name, dot, path = target.partition(".")
            if not separator or not dot:
                fail(f"--set-asset expects NAME.FIELD=VALUE: {assignment!r}")
            text = set_asset_field(text, name, path, value)
            updates.append((name, path, value))
        yaml_error = yaml.YAMLError if yaml is not None else ()
        try:
            manifest = parse_manifest(text)
        except yaml_error as error:
            fail(f"--set-asset produced an unparsable manifest: {error}")
        for name, path, value in updates:
            current: Any = manifest["assets"][name]
            for part in path.split("."):
                current = current[part]
            if not isinstance(current, str) or current != value:
                fail(f"assets.{name}.{path} did not update to the string {value!r}: {current!r}")
        outputs = render_asset_constants(manifest)
        manifest_path.write_text(text)
        write_outputs(outputs)
        print("asset pins updated: " + ", ".join(f"{name}.{path}" for name, path, _ in updates))
        return

    manifest = load_manifest()

    for path, content in outputs.items():

        remove_stale_generated_outputs(outputs)

def validate_codex_profile_modify_scripts(manifest: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    for name, profile in manifest.get("model_profiles", {}).items():
        path = ROOT / "home/dot_codex" / f"modify_private_{name}.config.toml"
        if not path.exists():
            fail(f"{path} is missing for model profile {name}")
        if path.stat().st_mode & 0o111 == 0:
            fail(f"{path} must be executable")
        result = subprocess.run(
            [str(path)],
            input="",
            text=True,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
            check=False,
        )
        if result.returncode != 0:
            fail(f"{path} must run successfully: {result.stderr.strip()}")
        profile_data = tomllib.loads(result.stdout)
        if profile_data.get("model") != profile.get("codex", {}).get("model"):
            fail(f"{path} must render the {name} profile model")
        if profile_data.get("model_reasoning_effort") != profile.get("codex", {}).get("model_reasoning_effort"):
            fail(f"{path} must render the {name} profile reasoning effort")
        if profile_data.get("sandbox_mode") != profile.get("codex", {}).get("sandbox_mode"):
            fail(f"{path} must render the {name} profile sandbox_mode override")
        if profile_data.get("features", {}).get("hooks") is not True:
            fail(f"{path} must enable hooks for the {name} profile")

def report_regime_boundary() -> None:

        ["bash", str(ROOT / "scripts/check-regime-boundary.sh"), "--report"],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )
    for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
        print(f"WARN: {line}", file=sys.stderr)
